# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/payouts/state-and-status/domestic-payout


State and Status

# Domestic Payout

## Pre-disbursement

Merchant Create Payout, but Insufficient Payout balance

PENDING

APPROVED

Option 1:

Re Top-up your Payout Balance

Retry the transactions in Dashboard > Domestic Payout > Approval

Option 2:

Set the transaction to FAILED on your end

Create new transactions with different Reference ID

Merchant Create Payout

IN PROGRESS

N/A

Waiting for the Payout Webhook

## Disbursement

Payout is in process by Partner

DONE

Retrieve Payout periodically to get the Final Status

Payout is successfully received by the Beneficiary Account

SUCCESS

Update the transaction status to SUCCESS on your end

## Post Disbursement

Payout is failed due to

The beneficiary account is invalid or doesn't exist

FAILED

Invalid Account

Do not retry the Transaction

Set transaction status to FAILED

Payout is failed due to Beneficiary account is inactive

Inactive Account

Payout is failed due to Beneficiary account is closed or deactivated

Dormant Account

Payout is failed due to Bank is failed to process the transaction

Failed to process by Bank Network

Can Retry the Transaction

Payout is failed due to Merchant is not eligible to access the feature at the moment

Feature not allowed at this time

Request to Pivot Team to enable the feature

Payout is failed due to Undefined Bank error response

Unknown Bank Network error

Payout is failed due to Beneficiary account has already reached the daily limit

Declined due to beneficiary limit

Can retry the Transaction tomorrow

# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/payouts/payout-capabilities/domestic-payout/processing-time


Payout Capabilities

Domestic Payout

# Processing Time

Most disbursements are processed instantly, with a maximum delay of 15 minutes. However, in certain cases, processing may follow the bank's cut-off schedule, which can take until outside cut-off hours.

Cut-off schedule

Transaction is requested during the cut-off time below will be held to PENDING , then we will continue to process the transaction after the cut-off time hours

```
PENDING
```

Mandiri

23.00 - 03.00

BNI

BRI

23.00 - 04.00

Bank Downtimes

All Indonesian banks experience routine maintenance periods, and these vary by bank. To minimize delays, we automatically route each disbursement through the fastest available channel. If a specific bank experiences prolonged downtime, Pivot will reroute the transaction through alternative banks whenever possible.

For disbursement requests made during scheduled bank downtimes (typically on Indonesian public holidays), we will notify you via WhatsApp group, specifying which beneficiaries are affected and which are not. This ensures your disbursements are processed at the earliest opportunity.

# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-information/authentication


API Information

# Authentication

Pivot uses OAuth to allow your application to gain programmatic access, request, and obtain permission from an account.

You need to send a request to get an access token to Pivot Server, then you will receive the access token that will expire in 900 seconds (15 minutes). Whenever it expires, you should send another request to get a new access token.

Tips!

You could create a cron job that generates an access token request that runs every 14 minutes, stores the access token in your system, and uses the access token for every next request.

## Access Token

Method and URL

POST [ BASE_URL ]/v1/access-token

```
POST
```

Request Header

X-MERCHANT-ID

[Your Client ID]

X-MERCHANT-SECRET

[Your Client Secret]

Request Body

```
{ " grantType " : " client_credentials " }
```

Response Body



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-information/idempotency


API Information

# Idempotency

Attach this Parameter to the Header Request whenever you create a Payment or Payout

X-REQUEST-ID

String

Unique Idempotency Key to make sure no double transaction created from Merchant

First-time request to create Payment / Payout using the X-REQUEST-ID : 123 , a successful process will return a Response HTTP Status 200

```
123
```

Second-time request to create Payment / Payout using the same X-REQUEST-ID : 123 With a different Payload, then Pivot will return HTTP Response 409

Second-time request to create Payment / Payout using the same X-REQUEST-ID : 123 With the same request Payload, then Pivot will return a HTTP Response Status 200, with the same initial response

The requirement for X-REQUEST-ID are:

Allow only alphanumeric characters

Minimum length is 16 digits

Maximum length is 36 digits

Expired in 24 hours, which means after 24 hours, you can reuse the same X-REQUEST-ID for another transaction request

API Information

# Pagination

Some API methods (e.g., Retrieve Payment Session Details & Retrieve a Payout ) use page-based pagination through page and perPage parameters. Both parameters can be set when hitting the endpoint. Below is the example:

```
page
```

```
perPage
```

GET [ BASE_URL ]/v2/payments/{id}?page=:page

```
GET
```

GET [ BASE_URL ]/v1/payouts/:id?page=:page&perPage=:perPage

[Page to be shown]

[Total record to be shown per page]

Maximum 100 records per page, default is 20 records



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-information/response


API Information

# Response

Response Handling for Pivot's API

## HTTP Status

Pivot uses standard and conventional HTTP Status Codes for each API Response given in a single API Request. In brief, the HTTP Status code 2xx indicates that the request is accepted and successfully processed by Pivot, 4xx indicating that the failure is caused by the information provided. (E.g., missing some required parameters, credentials are not valid). 5xx indicates that the error is on the Pivot side.

```
2xx
```

```
4xx
```

```
5xx
```

### HTTP Status List used in Pivot API

200 OK

The request is successful

400 Bad Request

Format / missing fields

401 Unauthorized

Invalid API key / token

403 Forbidden

Feature not enabled / blocked

404 Not Found

Invalid reference ID

422 Unprocessable Entity

Valid request, invalid state

429 Too Many Requests

Merchant-specific guard

500 Internal Server Error

Bug / unexpected failure

502 Bad Gateway

Invalid response from downstream

503 Service Unavailable

Downstream downtime

504 Gateway Timeout

No response within SLA

### Timeout

Pivot sets a default API timeout of 60 seconds. If the request takes longer than 60 seconds, then Pivot will return an HTTP code 408 for a timeout response.

### Response Format

Pivot API returns a uniform response format, which helps you traverse the response easily. The payload contains the following information:

code

String

A code that represents the success or failure of your request. The List of response codes can be found in each section of API Services

message

A human-readable format that explains the response

error

Object

Conditional

An extended payload that explains the error in detail. See Errors

data

Contains the response data related with the accessed resources

pagination

Contains pagination information such as page number, how many records shows in page, total records, and total pages available



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-information/errors


API Information

# Errors

Handling errors in Pivot's API

The error object explains the error details of your request. It is encapsulated as an error object in the response; if no error is found, then you should expect the error object to be omitted from the response.

### Error Object

```
{ " code " : " human-readable code response " , " message " : " human-readable message response " , " error " : { " type " : " API_ERROR " , " source " : " UPSTREAM " , " details " : [ { " field " : " email " , " message " : " Email is required " } ], " trace_id " : " ed9ef145530fc386190ddd7612ed61c7 " }, " data " : { // resource object }
```

The error object contains the following information:

Error Type values will be one of API_ERROR or GATEWAY_ERROR

API_ERROR : Either error is caused by a validation error or an invalid request. Usually will have an HTTP status 4xx

```
4xx
```

GATEWAY_ERROR : Error is on Pivot Gateway side. You should expect this to rarely happen. Usually will have an HTTP status 5xx

```
5xx
```

Error Source values will be one of UPSTREAM , DOWNSTREAM, or SYSTEM

UPSTREAM : Errors caused by merchant request, configuration, or business rules

DOWNSTREAM : Errors caused by external dependencies

SYSTEM : Errors caused by Pivot infrastructure or internal services

Error details are exclusive to validation errors. You may expect it will contain an array of invalid fields.

ID to trace your request, if a request is not accompanied by traceId , then Pivot's system will generate the trace ID for you. You can use the traceId when raising a helpdesk ticket to Pivot

```
traceId
```



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-information/callback


API Information

# Callback

Pivot will send real-time notifications to your system for every key event that occurs on each of your products. This ensures you always receive the latest status updates without needing to hit our API constantly.

You can register a dedicated Callback URL for each product directly from your dashboard:

Dashboard ? Setting ? Developer Settings ? Callbacks

For security, make sure to validate the Callback source using your Callback API Key , which is also available on the same Callbacks page.

## Delivery Retries

Pivot will automatically retry callback deliveries when your endpoint returns 5xx , 408 , 429 , or when internal service errors occur. These retry attempts help ensure important event notifications are not lost due to temporary issues on the merchant side.

```
408
```

```
429
```

Our retry schedule is as follows:

1st retry: after 5 minutes

2nd retry: after 15 minutes

3rd retry: after 1 hour

4th retry: after 3 hours

5th retry: after 6 hours

Final retry: after 12 hours

If all attempts fail, the callback will be flagged for manual retry .

You can manually trigger a retry anytime from the dashboard:

Dashboard ? Setting ? Developer Settings ? Callback History

Object

currency

# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/account-and-balance/sub-account-management/activity-on-behalf-of-sub-account


API Lists

Account and Balance

Sub-account Management

# Activity on behalf-of Sub-account

Perform actions or transactions as a sub-account using the main account?s credentials

Managing a Sub-account means you are in charge of its lifecycle, from registering a new Sub-account to blocking Sub-account activity. You may also be doing transactions on behalf of their account.

### Additional Header Value for activity on behalf of Sub-accounts

Add extra information in your API Request Header to execute any Action on behalf of your Sub-account. The requester might be coming from your system, but the action will be done on the Sub-account account.

x-submerchant-id

[Sub-account uuid]

name

String

1-25

Strin

0-255


# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/account-and-balance/sub-account-management/status


API Lists

Account and Balance

Sub-account Management

# Status

## Sub-account Status

KYC

CREATED

WAITING_FOR_DOCUMENT

Account created and still in inactive mode

Need to submit Sub-account KYC document

IN_REVIEW

Waiting for review results

REJECTED

Submit other Sub-accounts

ACTIVE

APPROVED

Account active and can do (any) activity via dashboard / API

DEACTIVATED

Main Account intentionally deactivated the Sub-account

BLOCKED

Account Blocked by Pivot due to any terms and policy violation

Contact our Helpdesk support

DORMANT

Account has not been have transaction in any balance (account) for a period of time

CLOSED

Account has been closed due to main account request

NON_KYC

NOT_REQUIRED

# Transfer Object

A transfer is a single money movement between accounts within the platform

```
{ " uuid " : " 8uh7-n332-9vst-l2c1 " , " referenceId " : " [your_reference] " , " recipientId " : " u734-981324saf-53tu " , " amount " : 100000 , " remarks " : " transfer for coffeeshop " , " transferType " : " DIRECT " , " status " : " SUCCESS " , " transaction_timestamp " : " 2024-05-13T08:21:44.967496538Z " , " created " : " 2024-05-13T08:21:44.967496538Z " , " updated " : " 2024-05-13T08:21:44.967496538Z " }
```

Detail Transfer Object

uuid

String

Object Unique Identifier

referenceId

Merchant Reference Id from the Client's System

recipientId

UUID of the Recipient, recipient can be a merchant for platform case or a customer for wallet case

amount

Number

Transfer amount

remarks

Transfer note or remarks from the Client's System

transferType

status

ENUM

Transfer Status, one of SUCCESS , PENDING and FAILED

```
SUCCESS
```

```
PENDING
```

```
FAILED
```

transactionTimestamp

Transfer transaction timestamp

created

Transfer Object created time in ISO-8601 Format. i.e. 2024-05-13T08:21:44.967496538Z

updated

Transfer Object last updated time in ISO-8601 Format. i.e. 2024-05-13T08:21:44.967496538Z

Object

possible values:

BALANCE_TRANSFER

PAYOUT_BALANCE

Boolean

false (default)

true

1-3


splitRoutingConfiguration

Array of Objects

merchantId

String

Route Payment destination, can route to Main Account or Sub-account within the platform

type

Type possible values:

FIXED

PERCENTAGE

currency

IDR

percentageAmount

Integer

Mandatory if the type value is "PERCENTAGE"

fixedAmount

Mandatory if the type value is "FIXED"

remarks



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/account-and-balance/response-code


API Lists

Account and Balance

# Response Code

## Generic API Error

400

API_ERROR

api_validation_error

The request was invalid, or an error occurred in downstream provider

User hit any endpoints with invalid payload

401

credentials_invalid

Access token is invalid, please verify that the authentication is provided and valid

User hit any endpoints but can't authenticate its API Access

403

resource_not_complete

Please verify that the setup is complete

User hit any endpoints but not set mandatory setup like redirect url, etc

forbidden_access

Provided API Key does not have the correct permissions to perform the operation

User is already authenticated but hit endpoints which out of scope of the authorization

404

GATEWAY_ERROR

not_found

The requested URL does not exist

User is already authenticated but hit wrong endpoints

resource_missing

The $resource with ID $id cannot be found

User is already authenticated but can't found the resources with specific ID

409

duplicate_error

There's already existing record with the provided details

User is already authenticated and send payload request which conflicted with existing resource

idempotency_error

The same Idempotency-key was provided with a different payload

User is already authenticated and send same idempotency key with different payload request, can be different data or different endpoint

429

frequency_above_limit

The frequency limit of $resource is reached for operation $operation

User hit same endpoints repeatedly many times in short period

500

internal_error

An internal error was encountered. Please Try again later

User is hit any endpoints but the pivot's server has encountered a problem

502

bad_gateway

503

service_unavailable

504

gateway_timeout

success

Access token is invalid

field_required

Mandatory Field is Missing

resource_already_exists

ID is already exists

invalid_recepient

Receepient ID is invalid

insufficient_fund

Insufficient balance for Transfer

resend_failed

Resend Invitation Failed: User Is Invalid

Resend Invitation Failed: User Already Active

topup_va_failed

Failed to Generate Virtual Account for Topup

assign_user_failed

Assign Admin User: Email Already Exist

general_error

General Error

example: [3, 6, 9, 12]

plans

example: [?regular?, ?0%?]

virtualAccount

PERMATA

BRI

MANDIRI

CIMB

BNC

etc

ewallet

DANA

SHOPEEPAY

OVO

Initiation

Enable "Save Payment Information Detail" option for your customers when the Charge is SUCCESS We will save your Customer's Payment Information Detail in Token format under your Customer Object

```
SUCCESS
```

How to build reference: Pay and Save Card (Tokenization)

Use Tokenized Card

Reuse your customers' saved Payment Information details so that they can check out faster

Stored Payment Method links to your customerId

```
customerId
```

How to build reference: Subscription and Automated Billing (Recurring)

Create Recurring

Create your plans, pricing, intervals, and trial settings: Create Recurring

First Authorization

Initiate the first Authorization of your Customer's Card Information

Authorize your Customer's Card Information using the Card Encryption flow: Card Customized Payment Page (Encryption)

Subsequent Transaction (Merchant managed scheduler)

Once the card is authorized and stored as a token, your scheduler is ready to charge the customer's bill

Recurring Plan & Stored Payment Method links to your customerId

Update Payment Method for ongoing Recurring Plan

You need to re-authorize your new Customer's Card Information without disrupting the ongoing Billing cycle. Default authorization is ONE_DOLLAR

```
ONE_DOLLAR
```

Utilize ongoing recurringId to update your customers' Payment Method

```
recurringId
```

### Virtual Account Payment Method

How to build reference: Build Static Payment

Make sure virtualAccountNumber within our default Static Range or your Configuration range:

```
virtualAccountNumber
```

https://dashboard.pivot-payment.com/static-payment/virtual-account/range

### QR Payment Method

### E-Wallet Payment Method

channel = DANA limitation:

```
channel
```

Does not support embedding a page using an iframe

Single Redirect URL handling for Success, Failure, and Expired cases. Pivot will use redirectUrl.successReturnUrl as a source

```
redirectUrl.successReturnUrl
```

Detail Parameter Request

clientReferenceId

Alphanumeric

1-100

Unique Payment Reference from Merchant's Request

amount

Object

Amount Object Total Payment amount

paymentType

String

Default to SINGLE

Payment Type possible values are:

SINGLE

MULTIPLE

Definition:

SINGLE : Payment Session can only be charged once

MULTIPLE : Payment Session can be charged multiple times, and Payment Session Status is always Active

```
Active
```

Applicable only for Payment Method Type QR and VIRTUAL_ACCOUNT

```
VIRTUAL_ACCOUNT
```

paymentMethod

Payment Method Object

paymentMethodOptions

Payment Method Options Object

saveForFutureUse

Boolean

Store Payment Information detail in Token format in Customer Object for CARD & EWALLET Payment method whenever the charge is SUCCESS

```
CARD
```

```
EWALLET
```

Default to false Possible values are:

true

false

showSavedPayment

Show saved Payment Information for CARD & EWALLET Payment method in Payment Redirection

Default to false, applicable only for REDIRECT mode Possible values are:

```
REDIRECT
```

mode

Default to REDIRECT

Payment Session mode. Possible values are:

REDIRECT will return redirectUrl

API

bypassStatusPage

Bypass Pivot's Status Page after your customer completing the Payment, directly redirect to your URLs

redirectUrl

Redirect Object URL for redirection

0-255

Auto generated based on email as the unique identifier *Choose either to send customerId or customer Object

customer

Customer Object Customer information *Choose either to send customerId or customer Object

orderInformation

Order Object Order information

autoConfirm

Default to true

If the confirmation method is set to false. Then clients need to hit the /confirm endpoint

statementDescriptor

0-20

Default = Merchant?s short name

Statement descriptors allow a maximum 20 characters for all the concatenated characters (including space).

It will be shown on invoice, receipt, and for payment methods that support custom descriptors, it will be shown on the customer's bank statement.

expirationMode

Default to LOOSE

LOOSE

STRICT

LOOSE : Expiry depends on processor rules

STRICT : Expiry depends on merchant rules

Applicable only for Payment Method Type CARD and EWALLET

expiryAt

Session expired time set by merchant with format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ. The default expiration time is 15 mins. For Payment Type = MULTIPLE , don't necessarily send the Expiry At value

Unique Recurring Reference Applicable for subscription and automated billing integration

initiateFirstAuthorization

Perform first authorization for new and updated Card information detail

Applicable for subscription and automated billing integration

Possible values are:

metadata

Free object for merchant to store any extra information about the payment session

Detail Parameter Response

Payment Session Object

# Cancel Payment Session

Void an unpaid payment session, preventing further transactions

## Method and URL

POST [ BASE_URL ]/v2/payments/{id}/cancel

```
POST
```

id from the Payment Session ID whenever you create Payment Session

Cancellation can be done if the Payment Session status is REQUIRE_CONFIRMATION or REQUIRE_PAYMENT_METHOD

```
REQUIRE_CONFIRMATION
```

```
REQUIRE_PAYMENT_METHOD
```

## Request

Request Body

```
{ " cancellationReason " : " REQUESTED_BY_CUSTOMER " }
```

Detail Parameter Request

cancellationReason

String

Reason to cancel Payment Session

## Response

Response Body

Detail Parameter Response

Object

Payment Session Object

```
POST
```

id from the Payment Session ID whenever you create Payment Session

```
{ " releaseRemainingAmount " : true , " amount " : { " currency " : " IDR " , " value " : 12000 } }
```

releaseRemainingAmount

Object

```
true
```

```
false
```

amount

Amount Object

Response Body

String

Unique Capture ID

paymentSessionId

PaymentSessionClientReferenceId

Alphanumeric

updatedAt

Updated time with format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ

```
POST
```

Request Body

```
{ " clientReferenceId " : " REF001 " , " mode " : " SELF_MANAGED " , " plan " : { " planId " : " PLAN001 " , " planName " : " Diamond " }, " amount " : { " currency " : " IDR " , " value " : 100000 }, " trials " : [ { " trialStart " : 1 , " trialEnd " : 1 , " type " : " FREE / FIXED / PERCENTAGE " , " percentage " : 50 , // applicable for trials.type = PERCENTAGE, maximum value is 100 " amount " : 50000 // applicable for trials.type = FIXED, maximum value is not greater than amount.value } ], " billingInterval " : 1 , " billingIntervalUnit " : " MONTH " , " endDate " : " 2026-08-31T16:59:59Z " , " firstAuthorization " : " FIRST_PAYMENT / ONE_DOLLAR " , " customerId " : " 019cff2f-b8ab-7b44-85e7-0c5d2d3c7a51 " , // applicable for existing customer " customer " : { // applicable for new customer " givenName " : " Reforza Jordan " , " surname " : " Geotama " , " email " : " reforza@pivot-payment.com " , " phoneNumber " : { " countryCode " : " +62 " , " number " : " 89557262933 " } } }
```

Detail Parameter Request

clientReferenceId

Alphanumeric

Unique Payment Reference from Merchant's Request

mode

String

Recurring scheduler management, default value to SELF_MANAGED

```
SELF_MANAGED
```

plan

Object

planId

Unique Plan Reference

planName

Plan Name

amount

Billing Amount Amount Object

trials

Array of Object

Trial Program from Merchant

trialStart

Integer

Initial trial start, default value to "1"

trialEnd

Count of trial end

type

Type of Trial scheme: 1. FREE 2. FIXED 3. PERCENTAGE

percentage

Number

Mandatory for trials.type = PERCENTAGE Maximum value is 100

```
trials.type
```

Mandatory for trials.type = FIXED Maximum value is not greater than amount.value

```
amount.value
```

billingInterval

Count of Billing Interval

billingIntervalUnit

Billing Interval Unit : 1. DAY 2. MONTH 3. YEAR

endDate

Recurring End Date with format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ

firstAuthorization

First Authorization method: 1. FIRST_PAYMENT 2. ONE_DOLLAR FIRST_PAYMENT = Authorized Amount based on the Billing Amount and Trials Program ONE_DOLLAR = Authorized Amount value is IDR 10.000, then Pivot will void the transaction to card owner limit

```
FIRST_PAYMENT
```

```
ONE_DOLLAR
```

customerId

Unique Customer Reference, Mandatory for existing Customer

customer

Customer information, Mandatory for new customer Customer Object

## Response

Response Body

Detail Parameter Response

recurringId

Unique Recurring Reference

Unique Customer Reference

AUTOMATIC (DEFAULT)

NEVER

CHALLENGE

EXTERNAL

processingConfig

Only for Facilitator model, to pass the bank MID they want to route this transaction to

bankMerchantId

0-50

merchantIdTag

threeDsInfo

Applicable for threeDsMethod = EXTERNAL

transactionId

1-50

Unique Transaction ID from authentication result

threeDsVersion

1-20

Indicates the 3DS version

eci

Possible values are:

transactionStatus

Y ? Authentication / Account verification successful

N ? Not authenticated / Account not verified; Transaction denied

U ? Authentication / Account verification could not be performed; technical or other problem

A ? Attempts processing performed; Not authenticated / verified, but a proof of attempt authentication / verification is provided

R ? Authentication / Account verification Rejected. Issuer is rejecting authentication / verification and request that authorization not be attempted

authenticationScheme

Card Principal possible values are:

VISA

MASTERCARD

JCB

AMEX

UNIONPAY

acsTransactionId

0-36

acsReference

0-32

authenticationTime

Authentication Time with format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ.

mcc

Merchant Category Code

cardOnFile

Recurring Payment for Network Token Authorization

initiator

Initiator possible values are:

CUSTOMER ? Cardholder is present / active (CIT); first subscription signup

MERCHANT ? System-triggered, no cardholder involvement (MIT); monthly recurring charge

type

Type possible values are:

SCHEDULED ? fixed amount at fixed intervals (Monthly subscription)

UNSCHEDULE ? variable amount or non-fixed intervals (e.g., auto top-up)

INSTALLMENT ? fixed number of charges splitting a single purchase

previousNetworkTransactionId

Mandatory if initiator = MERCHANT

```
MERCHANT
```

Network transaction ID from the original CIT, chained into all subsequent MITs.

installment

Installment Object

virtualAccount

channel

1-100

DANAMON

BNI

MANDIRI

BSI

BCA

BNC

CIMB

BRI

etc

virtualAccountName

0-100

All Virtual Account Name will use merchant short name

For some banks that can perform custom name, this field will be used

virtualAccountNumber

The corresponding virtual account number that can be used by Customers to send payments to

If merchants want to have their custom number they can pass this field within Static configuration range number in Dashboard Default Static Range: Limitation

expiryAt

VA expiration time

format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ For Payment Type = MULTIPLE , don't necessarily send the Expiry At value

```
MULTIPLE
```

ewallet

SHOPEEPAY

format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ

Detail Payment Session Object

String

Auto Generated

Unique ID of a payment session, generated by Pivot

clientReferenceId

Alphanumeric

1-100

Unique Payment Reference from Merchant's Request

amount

Amount Object Total Payment amount

autoConfirm

Boolean

Default to true

If the confirmation method is set to false. Then clients need to hit the /confirm endpoint

mode

Default to REDIRECT

Payment Session mode. Possible values are:

REDIRECT will return redirectUrl

API

bypassStatusPage

Bypass Pivot's Status Page after your customer completing the Payment, directly redirect to your URLs

Default to false, applicable only for REDIRECT mode Possible values are:

```
REDIRECT
```

true

false

redirectUrl

Redirect Object URL for redirection

paymentType

Default to SINGLE

Payment Type possible values are:

SINGLE

MULTIPLE

Definition:

SINGLE : Payment Session can only be charged once

MULTIPLE : Payment Session can be charged multiple times, and Payment Session Status is always Active

```
Active
```

Applicable only for Payment Method Type QR and VIRTUAL_ACCOUNT

```
VIRTUAL_ACCOUNT
```

paymentMethod

Payment Method Object

statementDescriptor

0-20

Default = Merchant?s short name

Statement descriptors allow a maximum 20 characters for all the concatenated characters (including space).

It will be shown on invoice, receipt, and for payment methods that support custom descriptors, it will be shown on the customer's bank statement.

expirationMode

Default to LOOSE

LOOSE

STRICT

LOOSE : Expiry depends on processor rules

STRICT : Expiry depends on merchant rules

Applicable only for Payment Method Type CARD and EWALLET

```
CARD
```

```
EWALLET
```

recurringId

Unique Recurring Reference Applicable for subscription and automated billing integration

initiateFirstAuthorization

Perform first authorization for new and updated Card information detail

Applicable for subscription and automated billing integration

Possible values are:

saveForFutureUse

Store Payment Information detail in Token format in Customer Object for CARD & EWALLET Payment method whenever the charge is SUCCESS

```
SUCCESS
```

Default to false Possible values are:

showSavedPayment

Show saved Payment Information for CARD & EWALLET Payment method in Payment Redirection

status

Status

investigationStatus

Applicable for the merchant who is eligible for Settlement Guarantee Program

Investigation Status:

INVESTIGATION_IN_PROCESS : Validating to our Bank Partner

```
INVESTIGATION_IN_PROCESS
```

INVESTIGATION_SUCCESS : Final Payment Status in our Bank Partner is SUCCESS

```
INVESTIGATION_SUCCESS
```

INVESTIGATION_FAILED : Final Payment Status in our Bank Partner FAILED

```
INVESTIGATION_FAILED
```

```
FAILED
```

createdAt

Session created time with format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ

updatedAt

Session latest updated time with format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ

expiryAt

Session expired time set by merchant with format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ. The default expiration time is 15 mins. For Payment Type = MULTIPLE , don't necessarily send the Expiry At value

encryptionKey

Only if :

paymentMethod.type = CARD

```
paymentMethod.type
```

mode = API

autoConfirm = false

To be used as Encryption of Customer's Card Information in Merchant FE

paymentUrl

Only If mode = REDIRECT

Pivot will generate a Payment URL for merchant to redirect their user to the page

chargeDetails

Array of object

Charge Object

Charged payments related to the payment session

customerId

0-255

Auto generated based on email as the unique identifier

customer

Customer Object Customer Information

orderInformation

Order Object Order Information

cancelledAt

Session cancellation time with format YYYY-MM-DDTHH:MM:SSZ

cancellationReason

Reason of cancellation, either provided by merchants or by Pivot System. Possible values

REQUESTED_BY_CUSTOMER - cancellation by end-customer

DUPLICATED - cancellation because of duplicated payment session

VOID_SESSION - cancellation by Merchant

CHARGE_FAILED - Session cancelled because user payment is rejected

FRAUDULENT - Session cancelled due to transaction marked as fraud

metadata

Free object for merchant to store any extra information about the payment session

# Response & Failure Code

## Generic API Error

200

Success

The request is successful

400

api_validation_error

The request was invalid, or an error occurred in downstream provider

API_ERROR

User hit any endpoints with invalid payload

Invalid BIN format

User hit an endpoints with invalid payload

401

credentials_invalid

Access token is invalid, please verify that the authentication is provided and valid

User hit any endpoints but can't authenticate its API Access

403

resource_not_complete

Please verify that the setup is complete

User hit any endpoints but not set mandatory setup like redirect url, etc

forbidden_access

Provided API Key does not have the correct permissions to perform the operation

User is already authenticated but hit endpoints which out of scope of the authorization

404

not_found

The requested URL does not exist

GATEWAY_ERROR

User is already authenticated but hit wrong endpoints

resource_missing

The $resource with ID $id cannot be found

User is already authenticated but can't found the resources with specific ID

data_not_found

BIN not found

User hit an endpoints with invalid data

409

duplicate_error

There's already existing record with the provided details

User is already authenticated and send payload request which conflicted with existing resource

idempotency_error

The same Idempotency-key was provided with a different payload

User is already authenticated and send same idempotency key with different payload request, can be different data or different endpoint

422

429

frequency_above_limit

The frequency limit of resource is reached for operation operation

User hit same endpoints repeatedly many times in short period

500

internal_error

An internal error was encountered. Please Try again later

User is hit any endpoints but the pivot's server has encountered a problem

502

bad_gateway

503

service_unavailable

504

gateway_timeout

card_decryption_failed

## Charge Failure

DECLINED_BY_CHANNEL

{{paymentSession.paymentMethod.type}} payment failed.

The transaction was declined by the channel

[[ if CARD ]]

[[ if EWALLET ]]

The user should contact their EWallet provider for clarification. The shopper can try again after resolving the issue with their issuer, or use another payment method.

IssuerAuthorizationCode

100

109

110

115

TBD

The transaction was declined by the issuer due to the card has already expired.

The shopper should try again with another valid card or use another payment method.

101

The transaction was declined by the issuer due to the submitted CVV is invalid.

INVALID_ACCOUNT

The transaction was declined by the issuer due to the card being marked as invalid.

The transaction was declined by the partner due to the account being marked as invalid.

The shopper should try again with another valid account or use another payment method.

111

AUTHENTICATION_FAILED

{{paymentSession.paymentMethod.type}} authentication failed.

The 3DS attempt was rejected by the issuer.

authenticationResult.threeDsResult = FAILED

SUSPECTED_FRAUD

The transaction was declined by the issuer due to the card being marked as stolen or potential fraud.

The card was reported as lost, the shopper should be validated for authenticity and be referred to their issuer.

The transaction was declined by channel due to the account being blocked or suspected as fraud.

The channel has declined the transaction due to suspicion of fraud, the shopper should be validated for authenticity and be referred to their issuer.

INSUFFICIENT_FUND

The transaction was declined by the issuer due to credit limit or balance is not sufficient.

[[ OTHER ]]

The transaction was declined due to the balance not sufficient or transaction limit exceeded.

Insufficient funds in the cardholder's account. The shopper can try again after adding funds to their bank account, or use another payment method.

Insufficient funds in the user?s account. The shopper can try again after adding funds to their account, or use another payment method.

116

121

CHANNEL_UNAVAILABLE

The transaction failed due to the issuer being unavailable or having a system malfunction.

The transaction failed due to the selected channel being unavailable or having a system malfunction.

The issuing bank cannot be contacted. The shopper should try again or use another payment method.

The channel cannot be contacted. The shopper should try again or use another payment method.

911

CANCELLED_BY_USER

The 3DS attempt was cancelled by the cardholder.

Payment cancelled by the user through the apps.

The shopper can try again or use another payment method

CHARGE_EXPIRED

{{paymentSession.paymentMethod.type}}

charge failed due to the transaction time has exceeded the channel expiration time.

3DS authentication expired

QR expired

VA number expired

# Status

## Payment Session Statuses

REQUIRE_PAYMENT_METHOD

Waiting for payment method

? By Merchant & Customer

Possible values:

REQUESTED_BY_CUSTOMER

DUPLICATED

VOID_SESSION

When Merchant created payment session and waiting for user to choose payment method

Choose payment Method

REQUIRE_CONFIRMATION

Waiting for confirmation

After payment method is selected, waiting for the payment session to be confirmed and continue to charge process

Confirm

REQUIRE_ACTION

Waiting for user action

? For API method

Payment method (Charge) already initiated and Waiting for customer action

Push Payment (VA & QR) = Customer to initiate transaction

Pull Payment (Card & Ewallet) = Customer to fill in details to be charged

ACTIVE

Waiting for charged

Multiple use payment type is already Active and Waiting for customer action

```
Multiple
```

Push Payment

(VA & QR) = Customer to initiate transaction

PROCESSING

Processing

Payment already processed by partners, charge is confirmed and already initiate the payment process

Wait for callback

[Final Status]

CANCELLED

Cancelled

Session cancelled by merchant / customer

Redirect to failed payment

EXPIRED

Expired

Customer exceed payment session time

INACTIVE

Inactive

Multiple use payment type is inactive

Create New Payment Session to generate Payment Type = MULTIPLE

```
MULTIPLE
```

PAID

Paid

Payment session successfully paid

Redirect to successful payment

## Charge Statuses

WAITING_FOR_USER_ACTION

Payment session is confirmed and waiting for customer to perform action

WAITING_FOR_AUTHENTICATION

Only applicable for pull payment

Waiting for partner to authenticate the transaction

Input OTP or PIN

Partner processing the transaction

Waiting for partner to complete the process

WAITING_FOR_CAPTURE

Only applicable for Cards and if autoCapture: false

Charge already authorized (money booked) but not captured yet

Merchant to call Capture

FAILED

Charge failed

Charge expired

SUCCESS

Charge is successful & completed

1-3

Boolean

false (default)

true

PENDING

DUPLICATE

CANCELLATION

OTHERS

description

0-50

Information describing the refund for merchant internal notes

destinationType

Value is available after refund reached to final status, indicating the refund destination OR if status = WAITING_BANK_TRANSFER then value will always be ACCOUNT

Possible values

CHANNEL ? fund is credited back to the payment channel used on the transaction

ACCOUNT ? fund is transferred to the submitted destination

method

Refund method preferred by Merchant / customer, possible values:

AUTO (default) ? Pivot will perform refund through Channel, if failed then fallback to Bank Transfer if available

TRANSFER_ONLY ? Refund will be performed through Bank Transfer only, Merchant is required to send the destination account

transferDestination

Required if preferredMethod = TRANSFER_ONLY

```
TRANSFER_ONLY
```

# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-local/local-payout-api-integration-guide


API Lists

Payout (Local)

# ?? Local Payout API Integration Guide

How to integrate Pivot Local Payout API

This guide explains the complete integration flow for sending local payouts using the Pivot Payment API. It covers authentication, beneficiary validation, payout creation, webhook handling, and payout status reconciliation.

This page provides an end-to-end workflow. For full endpoint specifications, refer to the individual API references.

### Overview

Pivot Local Payout API allows merchants to transfer funds from their Pivot balance to beneficiary bank accounts in Indonesia.

A typical payout integration consists of the following steps:

Authenticate API requests

Check available payout balance

Validate beneficiary bank account (Inquiry Account)

Create payout transaction

Receive payout status via webhook callback

Retrieve payout status for reconciliation (optional)

### Prerequisites

Before integrating the payout API, ensure that:

Your merchant account has been activated for Local Payout .

You have received the following credentials from Pivot:

Client Key

Used to authenticate requests

Client Secret

Used to sign requests

Merchant ID

Unique identifier for your account

You should also configure:

A secure webhook endpoint to receive payout callbacks

Proper idempotency handling to avoid duplicate payouts

### Base URL

Production

Sandbox

### Authentication

All API requests must include authentication headers. A valid access token must be included in the Authorization header when sending requests to the Pivot API.

```
Authorization
```

Endpoint reference:

Example headers:

Example request:

Example response:

Access tokens expire after 900 seconds (15 minutes) . You must request a new access token once the current token expires.

### Recommended Integration Flow

The recommended payout flow is shown below.

### Step 1 ? Check Payout Balance

Before creating a payout, ensure that your Pivot balance is sufficient to cover the transaction amount. If the balance is insufficient, the payout request will not be processed. The transaction will appear in the dashboard under Local Payout ? Need Action ? Waiting for Top Up , where you may retry the payout after topping up your balance or cancel the transaction.

Payout Balance

### Step 2 ? Validate Beneficiary Account

Before creating a payout, validate the beneficiary account using Inquiry Account .

This ensures:

the bank account exists

the account name matches the expected beneficiary

Inquiry Account

You should display the returned account_name to your user for confirmation.

### Step 3 ? Create Payout

Once the beneficiary is validated, create the payout transaction. You have two options to create a payout:

Using inquiryId, or

Using channel information

Create Payout

Example request using inquiryId:

Example request using channel information:

Note: For the full list of channel codes, refer to Channel Codes .

Important notes:

reference_id must be unique for each payout.

```
reference_id
```

Use idempotency to prevent duplicate transactions.

### Step 4 ? Handle Webhook Callback

Pivot sends a webhook callback when the payout status is updated.

Typical payout statuses:

PROCESSING

Payout is being processed

SUCCESS

Funds successfully transferred

FAILED

Payout failed

DELAYED

Payout is taking longer than expected to process

Example webhook payload for bulk payout:

Example webhook payload for single payout:

Your webhook endpoint should:

Verify the callback authenticity

Update payout status in your system

Return HTTP 200 to acknowledge receipt

If your endpoint returns an error or timeout, Pivot may retry sending the webhook. For more details on the retry mechanism, refer to Callback .

### Step 5 ? Retrieve Payout Status (Optional)

You may retrieve payout status via API for reconciliation or if webhook delivery fails.

Retrieve Payout

To retrieve the details of a specific payout, refer to Retrieve a Single Payout Request .

### Idempotency

To prevent duplicate transaction processing, all payout requests must include the X-REQUEST-ID header. Pivot uses this value to ensure that the same request is not processed multiple times.

```
X-REQUEST-ID
```

If the same X-REQUEST-ID is received more than once within the validity period, Pivot will treat the request as the same transaction and return the original response instead of creating a new payout.

The X-REQUEST-ID value must meet the following requirements:

Must contain only alphanumeric characters

Minimum length: 16 characters

Maximum length: 36 characters

The ID remains valid for 24 hours

After 24 hours , the same X-REQUEST-ID may be reused for a new transaction request.

Example Header:

Further measure to prevent duplicate payouts:

Do not retry payouts blindly if the transaction has not reached final status

Always check payout status before retrying

### Error Handling

Common payout failure scenarios include:

Invalid account

Beneficiary account is invalid or doesn't exist

Declined due to beneficiary limit

Beneficiary account has already reached the daily limit

Failed to process by Bank Network

Bank network failed to process the transaction

Refer to the Response Codes and Failure Reasons section for full error mappings.

### Common Integration Questions

If your webhook endpoint fails to respond with HTTP 200, Pivot will retry sending the callback. You should ensure your endpoint is highly available.

Do not retry immediately. Always retrieve the payout status first to avoid duplicate transfers.

Webhook is the primary mechanism for status updates. Retrieve payout status only for reconciliation or webhook failures.

Yes. This ensures the beneficiary account is valid and prevents payout failures.

Use a unique reference_id for each payout and implement idempotency logic in your system.

### API References

For full endpoint specifications, refer to:

Callback

Response Codes and Failure Reasons

```
{ " channelCode " : " BCA " , " channelInformation " : { " accountNumber " : " 999966660001 " , " accountName " : " Reforza Pivot " } }
```

Detail Parameter Request

channelCode

String

1-30

Channel code for payout destination such as Bank, E wallet or other channels

List of Channel code can be accessed here

Channel Code

channelInformation

Object

accountNumber

1-60

Account Number of payout destination

accountName

Account Name of payout destination from Merchant

## Response

Response Body

Detail Parameter Response

code

Response code

message

Response description

data

uuid

Unique Inquiry Account ID

merchantId

Unique merchant ID

inquiryResult

Status

Inquiry status to indicate the result

Potential value

VALID = requested account matched

WARNING = requested account is available, but the account might be mismatched. Please check detail for more information

INVALID = requested account is invalid or can?t be found

PENDING = inquiry account is still in progress

detail

Only available if the status is WARNING, INVALID, and PENDING explains the reason below:

Warning:

Account Name is not similar = "The account name entered does not match the bank's records. Please check the account information and try again. Bank record:{accountName from bank}"

Account Name is similar = ?The account name entered is not an exact match. Please check the account information and try again. Bank record: {accountName from bank}?

Invalid:

Account number not found.

Pending:

Inquiry in progress

## Tips! Storing Inquiry ID for future usage

After you have successfully received a response from the Inquiry Account, you can store the inquiryId .

```
inquiryId
```

So whenever you want to create a Payout to the same Beneficiary Account, you don't need to hit the Inquiry Account API again, just pass inquiryId on the payload here

Detail Payout Object

created

String

Timestamp when Payout request is made (using ISO 8601, Timezone UTC+0)

merchantId

Unique merchant ID given by Pivot

payoutsResults

Object

totalFailedAmount

Integer

Total Amount of all disbursement that has status Failed

totalFailedCount

The count of all disbursement that has status Failed

totalPendingAmount

Total Amount of all disbursement that has status Pending

totalPendingCount

The count of all disbursement that has status Pending

totalSuccessAmount

Total Amount of all disbursement that has status Success

totalSuccessCount

The count of all disbursement that has status Success

payouts

Array of Object

amount

value

Amount

E.g: 10000

currency

ISO 4217

Example:

Rupiah = IDR

channelCode

Channel code for payout destination such as Bank, E wallet or other channels

List of Channel code can be accessed Channel Codes

channelInformation

accountName

Account Name of payout destination

accountNumber

Account Number of payout destination

Timestamp when Payout transaction is made (using ISO 8601, Timezone UTC+0)

inquiryId

Inquiry ID returned by Pivot from inquiry bank account

description

Information that will be seen by beneficiary account in statement

Note:

Maximum length each channel code is 20 characters

Support alphanumeric characters only

referenceId

Merchant provide unique identifier for payout request

status

Status of Payout transaction :

APPROVED / REJECTED (current state always APPROVED in Open API)

PENDING

SUCCESS

FAILED

reason

Detail reason for FAILED Payout transaction status

Failure Reason

updated

Timestamp when Payout transaction status is updated (using ISO 8601, Timezone UTC+0)

Status of Payout request:

IN PROGRESS

DONE

Timestamp when Payout request status is updated (using ISO 8601, Timezone UTC+0)

uuid

Unique Bulk payout ID



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-local/response-codes-and-failure-reason


API Lists

Payout (Local)

# Response Codes & Failure Reason

## Response Codes

Success

credentials_invalid

Access Token is Invalid

field_format_invalid

Format Field is Invalid

field_required

Mandatory Field is Missing

amount_below_limit

Amount Value below the minimum

resource_already_exists

X-REQUEST-ID is already exists

ID is already exists

service_unavailable

Gateway / Partner service is unavailable

balance_insufficient

Merchant Balance is Insufficient

unsupported_channel_code

Channel Code is currently not supported

resource_missing

Payout ID is not exist

format_invalid

Payout format is invalid

Inquiry ID is not exist

general_error

General Error

amount_above_limit

Amount value above the maximum

daily_payout_limit_exceeded

Exceed daily Payout limit

payouts_in_process

Payouts are being process

Payouts format is invalid

field_value_invalid

Format Field is invalid

unprocessable_entity

disbursement status has not been approved

## Failure Reason

Failure reason will appear whenever Payout Transaction Status is "FAILED"

Invalid account

Beneficiary account is invalid or doesn't exist

Inactive account

Beneficiary account is inactive

Dormant account

Beneficiary account is closed or deactivated

Failed to process by Bank Network

Bank is failed to process the transaction

Feature not allowed at this time

Merchant is not eligible to access the feature at the moment

Unknown Bank Network error

Undefined Bank error response

Declined due to beneficiary limit

Beneficiary account has already reached the daily limit

# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international


API Lists

# Payout (International)

Send in Rupiah , and your recipient receives the full amount in local currency ? faster, safer, and simpler.

How it works:

Initiate global payouts via API or dashboard

Send money in IDR ? no hidden FX surprises

Recipient gets full amount in their local currency

## API Flow



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international/get-fx-rate


API Lists

Payout (International)

# Get FX Rate

## Method and URL

GET [ BASE_URL ]/xb/fx-rate

```
GET
```

## Request Params

destinationCurrency

{{destinationCurrency}}

sourceCurrency

IDR

## Response

Response Body

```
{ " code " : " 00 " , " message " : " Success " , " data " : { " fxRate " : " 0.000066142872 " , " destinationFxRate " : " 15118.7871007476058796 " , " expiryAt " : " 2024-09-27T09:00:00Z " } }
```

Detail Parameter Response

code

String

Response code

message

Response description

data

Object

fxRate

Conversion rate from source currency to destination currency

destinationFxRate

Conversion rate from destination currency to source currency

expiryAt

FX rate object expired time in ISO-8601 format. i.e. 2024-10-02T08:36:44.967496538Z

POST [ BASE_URL ]/xb/sender

```
POST
```

## Request Params

Request body

```
{ " name " : " Indonesah Newjeans " , " countryCode " : " ID " , " state " : " Jawa Barat " , " city " : " Bandung " , " address " : " Jl Street " , " postcode " : " 12345 " , " accountType " : " Individual " , " dob " : " 1990-12-01 " , // conditional, only for individual " bankAccountNumber " : " 98723293 " , // conditional, only for beneficiary country Korea " identificationType " : " Passport " , " identificationNumber " : " X123457 " , " contactCountryCode " : " +62 " , " contactNumber " : " 123134123 " , " sourceOfIncome " : " Salary " }
```

## Response

Response Body



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international/sender/update-sender


API Lists

Payout (International)

Sender

# Update Sender

## Method and URL

PUT [ BASE_URL ]/xb/sender/:id

```
PUT
```

## Request Params

Request body

```
{ " name " : " Indonesah Newjeans " , " countryCode " : " ID " , " state " : " Jawa Barat " , " city " : " Bandung " , " address " : " Jl Street " , " postcode " : " 12345 " , " accountType " : " Individual " , " dob " : " 1990-12-01 " , // conditional, only for individual " bankAccountNumber " : " 98723293 " , // conditional, only for beneficiary country Korea " identificationType " : " Passport " , " identificationNumber " : " X123457 " , " contactCountryCode " : " +62 " , " contactNumber " : " 123134123 " , " sourceOfIncome " : " Salary " }
```

## Response

Response Body

Sender Object

uuid

String Generated

Object unique identifier

merchantId

Merchant unique identifier

referenceId

String

Merchant remitter reference ID

name

Remitter name ^[A-Za-z0-9 -]{1,40}$

Max 40

Can only contain the special characters

countryCode

Enum

Remitter two-letter country code based on ISO 3166-1

state

Remitter state Length: Max 255

city

Remitter city Length: Max 35

address

Remitter address Length: Max 200

postcode

Number

Remitter postcode ^[a-zA-Z0-9\s\-]{0,16}$

Max 16

Can only contain the special characters -

accountType

Remitter account type (individual or company)

Value:

Should be either

Individual

Company

bankAccountNumber

C Required if beneficiary country code is KR

Remitter bank name

Length: Max 50

nationality

Remitter nationality in two-letter country code based on ISO 3166-1

Value: Can be the same as remitterCountryCode

identificationType

Remitter ID type according to account type

If Individual, should be either

Kartu Tanda Penduduk

Passport

If Company, should be either

Business Registration Number

ACRA

Travel Agent License Number

ABN

ACN

ARBN

identificationNumber

Remitter ID number based on the ID type selected ^[A-Za-z0-9\s-.]{1,30}$

Max 30

dob

Remitter date of birth

Required if account type is Individual

Format: YYYY/MM/DD

contactCountryCode

Remitter contact country code Length: Max 5

contactNumber

Remitter contact number

^[0-9+/-\\s]{0,17}$

Length: Max 17

sourceOfIncome

Remitter source of income

Salary

Personal Savings

Personal Wealth

Retirement Funds

Business Owner

Shareholder

Loan Facility

Personal Account

Corporate Account

# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international/object


API Lists

Payout (International)

# Object



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international/object/fx


API Lists

Payout (International)

Object

fxRate

String

Conversion rate from source currency to destination currency

destinationFxRate

Conversion rate from destination currency to source currency

expiryAt

FX rate object expired time in ISO-8601 format. i.e. 2024-10-02T08:36:44.967496538Z



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international/object/sender


API Lists

Payout (International)

Object

# Sender

uuid

String Generated

Object unique identifier

merchantId

Merchant unique identifier

referenceId

String

Merchant remitter reference ID

name

Remitter name ^[A-Za-z0-9 -]{1,40}$

Max 40

Can only contain the special characters

countryCode

Enum

Remitter two-letter country code based on ISO 3166-1

state

Remitter state Length: Max 255

city

Remitter city Length: Max 35

address

Remitter address Length: Max 200

postcode

Number

Remitter postcode ^[a-zA-Z0-9\s\-]{0,16}$

Max 16

Can only contain the special characters -

accountType

Remitter account type (individual or company)

Value:

Should be either

Individual

Company

bankAccountNumber

C Required if beneficiary country code is KR

Remitter bank name

Length: Max 50

nationality

Remitter nationality in two-letter country code based on ISO 3166-1

Value: Can be the same as remitterCountryCode

identificationType

Remitter ID type according to account type

If Individual, should be either

Kartu Tanda Penduduk

Passport

If Company, should be either

Business Registration Number

ACRA

Travel Agent License Number

ABN

ACN

ARBN

identificationNumber

Remitter ID number based on the ID type selected ^[A-Za-z0-9\s-.]{1,30}$

Max 30

dob

If account type is Individual: Remitter date of birth

If account type is Company: Company's date of incorporation

Format: YYYY/MM/DD

contactCountryCode

Remitter contact country code Length: Max 5

contactNumber

Remitter contact number

^[0-9+/-\\s]{0,17}$

Length: Max 17

sourceOfIncome

Remitter source of income

Salary

Personal Savings

Personal Wealth

Retirement Funds

Business Owner

Shareholder

Loan Facility

Personal Account

Corporate Account



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international/object/beneficiary


API Lists

Payout (International)

Object

# Beneficiary

uuid

String Generated

Object unique identifier

merchantID

Merchant unique identifier

referenceID

String

Merchant beneficiary reference ID

name

Beneficiary name ^[a-zA-Z\s]{1,70}$

Length Max 70

countryCode

Enum

Beneficiary two-letter country code based on ISO 3166-1

Value:

Should be either

state

Beneficiary state Length: Max 50

city

Beneficiary city Length: Max 35

address

Beneficiary address ^.{1,200}$

Length Max 200

postcode

Number

Beneficiary postcode Length: Max 15

accountType

Beneficiary account type (individual or company)

Value Should be either

Individual

Company

bankName

Beneficiary bank name ^[A-z0-9., -]{1,100}$

Max 100

Only allows special characters . , -

bankCode

C Required if beneficiary country is KR

Beneficiary bank code

Length: Max 3

accountNumber

Beneficiary bank account number

contactCountryCode

Beneficiary contact country code

Length: Max 5

contactNumber

Beneficiary contact number

^[0-9+/-\s]{0,17}$

Max 17

Can contain + - space numbers

email

Beneficiary email



# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international/object/international-payout


API Lists

Payout (International)

Object

# International Payout

uuid

String Generated

Object unique identifier

merchantId

Merchant unique identifier

referenceId

String

Merchant payout reference ID

remitterId

Remitter ID

beneficiaryId

Beneficiary ID

sourceCurrency

Enum

Three-letter source currency code based on ISO 4217 Value: Should be IDR

destinationCurrency

Three-letter destination currency code based on ISO 4217

destinationAmount

String Required

Amount to be received by beneficiary in destination currency

For others

Max decimal 2

Min amount 1

For KR & JP

Max decimal 0

Max amount KRW 5,500,000

For US

Max amount USD 1,000,000

purposeCode

Purpose of payout Value:

Should be either

IR001

IR002

IR003

IR004

IR005

IR006

IR007

IR008

IR009

IR010

IR011

IR012

IR013

IR014

IR015

IR016

IR017

IR020

IR01801

IR01802

IR01803

IR01804

IR01805

IR01806

IR01807

IR01808

IR01809

IR01810

IR01811

remarks

Payout remarks ^[a-zA-Z0-9_\s]+$

Max 20

Can only include special character _

routingValue

SWIFT or ACH or BSB code, depending on the selected country and currency

For SG, MY, TH, JP, CN:

To local currency: SWIFT Code

To foreign currency: SWIFT Code

For AU:

To local currency: BSB Code

For US:

To local currency: ACH Code

For KR:

To local currency: No code required

To foreign currency: No code required

SWIFT Codes

^[A-Za-z0-9]{8}$|^[A-Za-z0-9]{11}$

Length: 8 or 11

Special Character: None allowed

BSB Code

^[0-9]{6}$ , ^[A-Za-z0-9]{8}$|^[A-Za-z0-9]{11}$

Length: 6

ACH Code

/^[0-9]{9}$/

Length: 9

cnaps

Mandatory if destination currency is CNY and payout method is BANK_ACCOUNT

Length: 12

fxRate

Number Generated

Conversion rate from source currency to destination currency

destinationFxRate

Conversion rate from destination currency to source currency

fee

Payout fee

totalAmount

Total amount to be paid

createdAt

Payout object created time in ISO-8601 format. i.e. 2024-10-02T08:21:44.967496538Z

expiredAt

Payout object expired time in ISO-8601 format. i.e. 2024-10-02T08:36:44.967496538Z

status

Enum Generated

Payout status

payoutMethod

Specify as WALLET if want to perform payout to Alipay

If not specified, then default will be BANK_ACCOUNT

# Source: https://pivot-payment.gitbook.io/pivot-docs/api-references/api-lists/payout-international/object/payout-status


API Lists

Payout (International)

Object

# Payout Status

WAITING_FOR_CONFIRMATION

Waiting for merchant to confirm payout

INSUFFICIENT_BALANCE

Payout confirmed but merchant?s balance is insufficient

EXPIRED

Payout session has expired and merchant needs to create a new payout session

PENDING

Payout request sent

DOCUMENT_REQUESTED

Further information (RFI) requested by compliance

IN_REVIEW

RFI submitted and in review by compliance

PARTNER_REJECTED

Payout rejected by partner?s compliance

PROCESSING

Payout in process and instruction being sent to the bank

BENEFICIARY_REJECTED

Payout rejected by beneficiary through bank

FAILED

Payout failed

REFUNDED

Payout refunded

SUCCESS

Payout successful
